- **40:1** "The twenty-fifth year of our exile, at the beginning of the year, on the tenth day of the month" is April 573 before Christ, and "the fourteenth year after the city was struck down" is the same year. "At the beginning of the year" translates *be-rosh ha-shanah*.
- **40:2** "Visions of God" translates *mar'ot Elohim*, as in 1:1 and 8:3. "A very high mountain" translates *har gavoah me'od*, and "a structure like a city to the south" translates *ke-mivneh ir mi-negev*.
- **40:3** "A line of flax" translates *petil pishtim*, and "a measuring reed" translates *qaneh ha-middah*.
- **40:5** "Six long cubits, each a cubit and a handbreadth" translates *shesh ammot ba-ammah ve-tefach*. The wall is the wall of the temple area.
- **40:6** The verses 6 to 16 are hard in the Hebrew. "The gateway" translates *sha'ar*, "the threshold" translates *saf*, "the side rooms" translates *ta'im*, "the vestibule" translates *ulam*, "the jambs" translates *eilim*, and "windows ... narrowing inwards" translates *challonot atumot*. The terms are not all known.
- **40:17** "Chambers" translates *lishkot*, and "a pavement" translates *ritspah*.
- **40:22** "Seven steps" translates *sheva ma'alot*.
- **40:38** "A chamber" translates *lishkah*, where the burnt offering was washed.
- **40:39** "The burnt offering", "the sin offering" and "the guilt offering" translate *olah*, *chatta't* and *asham*.
- **40:43** "Hooks" translates *shefattayim*, a rare word that is hard.
- **40:46** "The sons of Zadok" translates *benei Tsadoq*.
- **41:1** "The nave" translates *heikhal*, and "the jambs" translates *eilim*.
- **41:3** "The inner room" translates *ha-penimah*.
- **41:4** "The Most Holy Place" translates *qodesh ha-qodashim*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **41:5** "The side chambers" translates *tselot*.
- **41:6** The verses 6 to 12 are hard in the Hebrew.
- **41:8** "A raised platform" translates *gavoah*.
- **41:12** "The yard" translates *ha-gizrah*, and "the building" translates *ha-binyan*.
- **41:16** "The galleries" translates *attiqim*.
- **41:18** "Cherubim and palm trees" translates *keruvim ve-timmorim*.
- **41:21** "The doorposts ... were squared" translates *mezuzat ha-heikhal revu'a*. "The Holy Place" translates *ha-qodesh*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **41:22** "An altar of wood" translates *mizbach etz*, and "the table that is before the LORD" translates *ha-shulchan asher lifnei YHWH*.
- **41:25** "A canopy" translates *av*.
- **42:3** "Gallery against gallery" translates *attiq el-pnei attiq*, which is hard.
- **42:13** "The holy chambers" translates *lishkot ha-qodesh*. See entry H-QADOSH. The word "most holy" translates *qodesh ha-qodashim*.
- **42:14** "The holy place" translates *ha-qodesh*, and "for these are holy" translates *ki-qodesh hennah*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **42:16** "Five hundred reeds" is as the Hebrew has it, and the Greek has "five hundred cubits". The verse 20 has "five hundred long and five hundred broad" without a unit, and 45:2 has five hundred cubits.
- **42:20** "A separation between the holy and the common" translates *le-havdil bein ha-qodesh le-chol*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **43:2** "Like the sound of many waters" translates *ke-qol mayim rabbim*, as in 1:24.
- **43:3** "When I came to destroy the city" translates *ke-vo'i le-shachet et-ha-ir*, in which the words mean the coming of the prophet to announce the destruction. The Greek and the Latin have "when he came".
- **43:5** "The spirit" translates *ruach*. See entry H-RUACH.
- **43:7** "At their high places" follows the reading tradition (*bamotam*). The written text has "in their death" (*bemotam*). "The soles of my feet" translates *kappot raglai*.
- **43:8** "By setting their threshold by my threshold" translates *be-titto sippam et-sippi*.
- **43:9** "The dead bodies of their kings" translates *pigrei malkheihem*, and the word *peger* may also mean a memorial offering.
- **43:10** "That they may be ashamed" translates *ve-yikkalmu*.
- **43:11** "Its teachings" translates *torotav*. See entry H-TORAH.
- **43:12** "The teaching of the temple" translates *torat ha-bayit*. See entry H-TORAH. "Most holy" translates *qodesh qodashim*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **43:13** The words for the parts of the altar (*chek*, *gevul*, *zeret*) are rare.
- **43:15** "The altar hearth" translates *ha-har'el*, and "horns" translates *qarnot*.
- **43:16** "The ledge" translates *azarah*.
- **43:20** "Make atonement for it" translates *ve-chitte'tho ve-kipparto*. See entry H-KAPPER.
- **43:21** "The appointed place" translates *mifqad*, and "the sanctuary" translates *ha-miqdash*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **43:26** "Make atonement" translates *yekapperu*. See entry H-KAPPER. "Consecrate" translates *u-millu yadav*, literally "fill its hand".
- **43:27** "Your peace offerings" translates *shalmeikhem*, from *shelem*, and is not tagged.
- **44:1** "The outer gate of the sanctuary" translates *sha'ar ha-miqdash ha-chitson*. See entry H-QADOSH. "Shut" translates *sagur*.
- **44:3** "The prince" translates *ha-nasi*.
- **44:5** "Mark well" translates *sim libbekha*. "All its teachings" translates *kol-torotav*. See entry H-TORAH.
- **44:7** "Foreigners, uncircumcised in heart and flesh" translates *benei nekhar arelei lev ve-arelei basar*. "My food" translates *lachmi*. "You have broken my covenant" translates *va-yaferu et-beriti*. See entry H-BERIT.
- **44:10** "The Levites" translates *ha-Leviyyim*.
- **44:11** "Ministers" translates *meshartim*.
- **44:12** "A stumbling block of iniquity" translates *mikhshol avon*.
- **44:13** "My holy things" translates *qodashai*, and "the things that are most holy" translates *qodshei ha-qodashim*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **44:15** "The sons of Zadok" translates *benei Tsadoq*.
- **44:17** "Linen" translates *pishtim*, and "wool" translates *tsemer*.
- **44:18** "Linen caps" translates *pa'arei pishtim*, and "anything that causes sweat" translates *ba-yaza*.
- **44:19** "Lest they communicate holiness to the people" translates *ve-lo-yeqaddeshu et-ha-am*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **44:20** "They will not shave their heads" translates *lo yegallechu et-ro'sham*.
- **44:23** "The holy and the common" translates *bein qodesh le-chol*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **44:24** "My teachings" translates *torotai*. See entry H-TORAH. "They will keep my Sabbaths holy" translates *yeqaddeshu*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **44:27** "The holy place" translates *ha-qodesh*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **44:28** "Inheritance" translates *nachalah*, and "possession" translates *achuzzah*.
- **44:29** "Every devoted thing" translates *kol-cherem*.
- **44:30** "Firstfruits" translates *bikkurim*, "contributions" translates *terumot*, and "dough" translates *arisot*.
- **44:31** "A carcass or torn by beasts" translates *nevelah u-terefah*.
- **45:1** "A holy portion" translates *terumah qodesh*. See entry H-QADOSH. "Twenty thousand" is as the Hebrew has it, and the Greek has "ten thousand", as in 45:3 and 48:9.
- **45:2** "The sanctuary" translates *ha-miqdash*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **45:3** "The Most Holy Place" translates *qodesh ha-qodashim*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **45:5** "Twenty chambers" is as the Hebrew has it (*esrim lishkot*), and the Greek has "cities to live in". The verse is hard.
- **45:6** "The possession of the city" translates *achuzzat ha-ir*.
- **45:8** "My princes" translates *nesi'ai*.
- **45:9** "Justice and righteousness" translates *mishpat u-tsedaqah*. See entry H-TSEDAQAH. "Evictions" translates *gerushot*.
- **45:10** "Just balances" translates *moznei tsedeq*, "a just ephah" translates *efat tsedeq*, and "a just bath" translates *bat tsedeq*. See entry H-TSEDAQAH.
- **45:11** The *ephah* is a measure of dry goods, the *bath* a measure of liquids, and the *homer* is ten of each.
- **45:12** "Twenty shekels plus twenty-five shekels plus fifteen shekels will be your mina" is hard in the Hebrew.
- **45:13** "The contribution" translates *ha-terumah*.
- **45:15** "To make atonement" translates *le-khapper*. See entry H-KAPPER. "Peace offerings" translates *shelamim*, and is not tagged.
- **45:17** "To make atonement" translates *le-khapper*. See entry H-KAPPER.
- **45:18** "Purify the sanctuary" translates *ve-chitte'ta et-ha-miqdash*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **45:20** "On the seventh day of the month" follows the Hebrew, and the Greek has the same. "Through error or ignorance" translates *mi-shogeh u-mi-peti*. "You will make atonement" translates *ve-khipparten*. See entry H-KAPPER.
- **45:21** "The Feast of the Passover" translates *chag ha-Pesach*.
- **45:25** "The feast" translates *ba-chag*, and is the festival of the seventh month (see Leviticus 23:33-43).
- **46:1** "The six working days" translates *sheshet yemei ha-ma'aseh*.
- **46:2** "Bow down" translates *ve-hishtachavah*.
- **46:12** "A freewill offering" translates *nedavah*.
- **46:14** "A perpetual statute" translates *chuqqot olam*.
- **46:17** "The year of liberty" translates *shenat ha-deror*. See also Leviticus 25:10.
- **46:19** "The holy chambers" translates *lishkot ha-qodesh*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **46:20** "Communicate holiness" translates *le-qaddesh*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **46:22** "Small courts" translates *chatserot qetturot*, which is hard.
- **46:23** "A row of masonry" translates *tur*, and "hearths" translates *mevashshelot*.
- **46:24** "Kitchens" translates *beit ha-mevashshelim*.
- **40:1** "The twenty-fifth year of our exile, at the beginning of the year, on the tenth day of the month" is April 573 before Christ, and "the fourteenth year after the city was struck down" counts from the fall of Jerusalem in 587 or 586.
- **40:2** "In visions of God" translates *be-mar'ot Elohim*, and "a structure like a city" translates *ke-mivneh ir*.
- **40:3** "A linen cord" translates *pethil pishtim*, and "a measuring reed" translates *qeneh ha-middah*.
- **40:5** "A cubit and a handbreadth" translates *ammah ve-tefach*, the long cubit, about 52 centimetres, and the reed is six of these.
- **40:6** The verses 6 to 16 on the east gate are hard, and the words for the parts of the gate are rare.
- **40:16** "Windows, narrowing inwards" translates *chalonot atumot*.
- **40:17** "Chambers" translates *lishkot*, and "a pavement" translates *ritspah*.
- **40:38** "Rinsed" translates *yadichu*.
- **40:39** "The burnt offering", "the sin offering" and "the guilt offering" translate *olah*, *chatta't* and *asham*. They are rendered as written, with no comment.
- **40:46** "The sons of Zadok" translates *benei Tsadoq*. It is rendered as written, with no comment.
- **41:1** "The nave" translates *ha-hekhal*, and "the tent" translates *ha-ohel*, which is hard.
- **41:4** "The Most Holy Place" translates *qodesh ha-qodashim*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **41:6** The verses 6 and 7 on the side chambers are hard.
- **41:21** "The Holy Place" translates *ha-qodesh*, and the verse is hard. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **41:22** "The table that is before the LORD" translates *ha-shulchan asher lifnei YHWH*.
- **42:13** "The holy chambers" translates *lishkot ha-qodesh*, and "the most holy offerings" translates *qodshei ha-qodashim*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **42:14** "These are holy" translates *ki qodesh hennah*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **42:16** "Five hundred reeds" follows the Hebrew (*qanim*), and the Greek has "cubits". The same is in 42:17, 42:18 and 42:19.
- **42:20** "A separation between the holy and the common" translates *le-havdil bein ha-qodesh le-chol*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **43:2** "The glory of the God of Israel" translates *kevod Elohei Yisra'el*.
- **43:3** "I fell on my face" is as in 1:28.
- **43:5** "The Spirit" translates *ruach*. See entry H-RUACH. The capital letter is the draft's choice.
- **43:7** "The place of my throne" translates *meqom kis'i*, and "the place of the soles of my feet" translates *meqom kappot raglai*. "At their death" follows the Hebrew *be-motam*, and the same letters may be read *bamotam*, "in their high places", and the Greek has a different reading.
- **43:12** "The law of the temple" translates *torat ha-bayit*, and the same is said twice. See entry H-TORAH. "Most holy" translates *qodesh qodashim*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **43:13** "A cubit and a handbreadth" is as in 40:5.
- **43:15** "The altar hearth" translates *har'el*, and in verse 16 *ariel*.
- **43:19** "The Levitical priests of the family of Zadok" is rendered as written, with no comment.
- **43:20** "You will take some of its blood" and "you will purify the altar and make atonement for it" translate *ve-chitte'tu ... ve-kipparto*. See entry H-KAPPER.
- **43:26** "They will make atonement" translates *yekapperu*. See entry H-KAPPER. "Consecrate" translates *u-millu yadav*, literally "fill its hand".
- **43:27** "Your peace offerings" translates *shalmeikhem*.
- **44:1** "He brought me back" translates *va-yeshivi'ni*. See entry H-SHUV. "The sanctuary" translates *ha-miqdash*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **44:3** "The prince" translates *ha-nasi*.
- **44:7** "Uncircumcised in heart and flesh" translates *arelei-lev ve-arelei basar*, and "my covenant" translates *beriti*. See entry H-BERIT.
- **44:10** "Shall bear their punishment" translates *ve-nasu avonam*.
- **44:15** "The Levitical priests, the sons of Zadok" is rendered as written, with no comment.
- **44:17** "Linen" translates *pishtim*.
- **44:19** "They transmit holiness" translates *ve-lo yeqaddeshu*, and "the holy chambers" translates *lishkot ha-qodesh*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **44:22** "Virgins" translates *betulot*. The rendering of *betulah* is open to the team's decision.
- **44:24** "My laws" translates *torotai*. See entry H-TORAH. "They will keep my Sabbaths holy" translates *yeqaddeshu*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **44:27** "The Holy Place" translates *ha-qodesh*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **44:28** "Their inheritance" translates *nachalatam*.
- **44:30** "The first of your dough" translates *arisotekhem*.
- **45:1** "A holy district" translates *terumah qodesh*, and "cubits" is supplied in the numbers that follow, as the Greek has it. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **45:2** "The sanctuary" translates *ha-miqdash*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **45:3** "The Most Holy Place" translates *qodesh qodashim*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **45:8** "My princes" translates *nesi'ai*.
- **45:9** "Justice and righteousness" translates *mishpat u-tsedaqah*. See entry H-TSEDAQAH. "Your evictions" translates *gerushoteikhem*.
- **45:10** "Just balances, a just ephah and a just bath" translates *moznei tsedeq ve-efat tsedeq u-vat tsedeq*. See entry H-TSEDAQAH.
- **45:11** The ephah is a dry measure and the bath a liquid measure, and the homer is ten of each.
- **45:12** "Twenty shekels, twenty-five shekels, fifteen shekels will make a mina" is hard in the Hebrew.
- **45:15** "To make atonement" translates *le-khapper*. See entry H-KAPPER.
- **45:17** "To make atonement" translates *le-khapper*. See entry H-KAPPER.
- **45:18** "Purify the sanctuary" translates *ve-chitte'ta et-ha-miqdash*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **45:20** "On the seventh day of the month" follows the Hebrew, and the Greek has "on the first day of the seventh month". "You will make atonement" translates *ve-kipparten*. See entry H-KAPPER.
- **45:21** "The Feast of the Passover" translates *chag ha-pesach*.
- **45:25** "In the seventh month, on the fifteenth day" is the feast of tabernacles.
- **46:1** "The six working days" translates *sheshet yemei ha-ma'aseh*.
- **46:9** "No one will return" translates *lo yashuv*. See entry H-SHUV.
- **46:12** "A freewill offering" translates *nedavah*.
- **46:14** "A statute for ever" translates *chuqqot olam*.
- **46:17** "The year of liberty" translates *shenat ha-deror*, and "it will revert" translates *ve-shavat*. See entry H-SHUV.
- **46:19** "The holy chambers" translates *lishkot ha-qodesh*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **46:20** "Transmit holiness" translates *le-qaddesh*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **46:22** "Small enclosed courts" translates *chatserot qetu'rot*, which is hard.
- **47:1** "He brought me back" translates *va-yeshivi'ni*. See entry H-SHUV.
- **47:3** "A measuring line" translates *qav*, and "ankle-deep" translates *mei afsayim*.
- **47:5** "Deep enough to swim in" translates *mei sachu*.
- **47:6** "He led me back" translates *va-yeshivheni*. See entry H-SHUV.
- **47:7** "As I went back" translates *be-shuvi*. See entry H-SHUV.
- **47:8** "The Arabah" translates *ha-Aravah*, and "the water of the sea will become fresh" translates *ve-nirpu ha-mayim*, literally "the waters will be healed".
- **47:10** "En-gedi" and "En-eglaim" are places on the shore of the sea.
- **47:12** "The sanctuary" translates *ha-miqdash*. See entry H-QADOSH. "Healing" translates *le-tarufah*.
- **47:13** "Joseph will have two portions" refers to Ephraim and Manasseh.
- **47:15** "The Great Sea" is the Mediterranean, and the places of the verses 15 to 17 (Hethlon, Lebo-hamath, Zedad, Berothah, Sibraim, Hazer-hatticon and Hazar-enon) are not all known.
- **47:22** "The foreigners" translates *gerim*, as in 22:7.
- **48:8** "The sanctuary" translates *ha-miqdash*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **48:10** "The holy portion" translates *terumat ha-qodesh*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **48:11** "The consecrated priests" translates *ha-kohanim ha-mequddash*. See entry H-QADOSH. The words on the sons of Zadok and the Levites are rendered as written, with no comment.
- **48:12** "A most holy place" translates *qodesh qodashim*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **48:14** "It is holy to the LORD" translates *ki-qodesh la-YHWH*. See entry H-QADOSH.
- **48:15** "Common use" translates *chol*.
- **48:35** "The LORD Is There" translates *YHWH shammah*, and "18,000 cubits" is the circumference of the city (4,500 on each of its four sides).

| 1:1-3 | Ezekiel 8:1-4; 10:1-22; 40:1-2; Jeremiah 1:1-3; 29:1; Isaiah 6:1-8; Genesis 28:12; Acts 7:55; Revelation 4:1 | Parallel and allusion: the heavens opened, and the word and the hand of the LORD |
| 1:4-14 | Psalm 18:7-15; 104:3-4; Isaiah 6:2-3; Ezekiel 10:1-22; Revelation 4:6-8 | Parallel and allusion: the stormy wind, the fire, and the four living creatures |
| 1:15-28 | Daniel 7:9-10; Isaiah 6:1; Exodus 24:10; Psalm 29:3; Genesis 9:13-16; Ezekiel 10:1; 43:2; Revelation 4:2-3; 10:1 | Parallel and allusion: the wheels, the dome, the throne, and the glory of the LORD |
| 2:1-10 | Jeremiah 1:7-10, 17-19; Isaiah 6:8-10; Daniel 8:17; Ezekiel 3:25-27; Revelation 5:1 | Parallel and allusion: the call, the rebellious house, and the scroll |
| 3:1-11 | Jeremiah 15:16; 1:8, 17; Psalm 19:10; 119:103; Isaiah 50:7; Ezekiel 2:3-7; Revelation 10:8-11 | Parallel and allusion: the scroll eaten, sweet as honey, and the hard forehead |
| 3:12-21 | Ezekiel 3:14; 8:3; 11:1, 24; 18:21-32; 33:1-20; Isaiah 62:6; Jeremiah 6:17; Hosea 9:8 | Parallel and allusion: the spirit that lifted him, and the watchman |
| 3:22-27 | Ezekiel 1:28; 8:4; 24:25-27; 33:21-22; Jeremiah 36:5 | Parallel and allusion: the valley, the cords, and the tongue that clings |
| 4:1-8 | Jeremiah 19:1-15; 13:1-11; 28:10-14; Isaiah 20:2-4; Leviticus 26:18-24; Numbers 14:34 | Parallel and allusion: the brick, the iron plate, and the iniquity borne |
| 4:9-17 | Leviticus 26:26; 11:39-40; 17:15; Deuteronomy 28:53-57; Hosea 9:3-4; Lamentations 2:11-12, 20; Acts 10:14 | Parallel and allusion: bread by weight and measure, and the staff of bread broken |
| 5:1-4 | Isaiah 7:20; Numbers 6:5, 18; Jeremiah 15:2; 43:11 | Parallel and allusion: the sword as a razor, and the hair in three parts |
| 5:5-17 | Leviticus 26:22-39; Deuteronomy 28:49-68; Jeremiah 19:9; Lamentations 4:10; Ezekiel 14:12-21 | Parallel and allusion: Jerusalem in the centre of the nations, and the judgements |
| 6:1-14 | Leviticus 26:30-31; Deuteronomy 12:2; Jeremiah 2:20; 3:6; 17:2; Hosea 4:13; Ezekiel 36:1-15 | Parallel and allusion: the mountains of Israel, the high places, and the remnant who remember |
| 7:1-9 | Amos 8:2; Habakkuk 2:3; Zephaniah 1:14-18; Isaiah 2:12 | Parallel and allusion: "an end" on the four corners of the land |
| 7:10-27 | Zephaniah 1:18; Proverbs 11:4; Isaiah 2:20; Jeremiah 6:14; 8:11; 18:18; Micah 3:11; Lamentations 2:9; Ezekiel 13:10; 22:26 | Parallel and allusion: the day of wrath, the silver and gold, and the vision sought in vain |
| 8:1-6 | Ezekiel 1:1-3, 26-28; 3:12-15; 40:1-2; Deuteronomy 32:16, 21; Jeremiah 7:30; 2 Chronicles 33:7, 15 | Parallel and allusion: the hand of the Lord GOD, the vision, and the image of jealousy |
| 8:7-18 | Deuteronomy 4:15-19; 17:3; Jeremiah 7:17-18; 44:15-19; 2:27-28; Isaiah 65:3-4; 66:17; 29:15; Psalm 94:7; Ezekiel 9:9 | Parallel and allusion: the carved idols, Tammuz, and the worship of the sun |
| 9:1-11 | Exodus 12:23; Zechariah 13:8-9; Jeremiah 25:29; 1 Peter 4:17; Revelation 7:2-4; 9:4; Ezekiel 8:6 | Parallel and allusion: the executioners, the man clothed in linen, and the mark |
| 10:1-22 | Ezekiel 1:4-28; 8:4; 9:3; 11:22-23; Isaiah 6:1-7; Exodus 25:18-22; Revelation 8:5 | Parallel and allusion: the cherubim, the wheels, the coals, and the glory over the threshold |
